- **user-sleep**: The user is going to sleep or stepping away and cannot answer anything until they return. Use this skill the moment the user says they are going to bed, signing off, going AFK, leaving for the night, "keep going while I'm gone", "don't wait for me", "have it done by morning", or otherwise hands over work to finish unattended. From that point on, never ask the user a question and never wait for input — make every decision with your best judgment and log it for their review. ([EricWang12/zw-claude-skills](https://github.com/EricWang12/zw-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/user-sleep/skills/user-sleep) · ★1)
